the year ended 31 December 2024 (the “year under review”).            業績。

market’s recovery, and consumer sentiment remained cautious.          應鏈整合，亦對製造業造成挑戰。

posed challenges to the manufacturing industry.

    MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
    管理層討論及分析



    REVIEW OF OPERATIONS                                                 業務回顧

    Although global inflationary pressures eased in 2024, the global     2024年，全球通脹壓力雖有所緩解，然而，
    economy continued to grow at a slow pace due to the uncertain        受到地緣政治局勢不明朗及高息環境等影
    geopolitical situation and the high interest rate environment. The   響，全球經濟持續緩慢增長。聯合國預計
    United Nations forecasted global economic growth of 2.8% in          2024年全球經濟增長率為2.8%，增速顯著
    2024, which remains well below the pre-pandemic levels. During       低於疫情前水平。年內，在發達經濟體中，
    the year, among the developed economies, the US economy              美國經濟按年增長2.8%，而零售銷售按年增
    grew by 2.8% year-on-year and retail sales increased by 4.4%         長 4.4%； 中 國 內 地 受 惠 於 擴 內 需 促 消 費 政
    year-on-year; Mainland China benefited from policies aimed           策，經濟按年增長5.0%，而社會消費品零售
    at expanding domestic demand and boosting consumption,               總 額 按 年 增 長 3.5%； 歐 元 區 國 內 生 產 總 值
    resulting in economic growth of 5.0% year-on-year and a 3.5%         (GDP)按年增長0.7%，而12月零售銷售按年
    year-on-year increase in total retail sales of social consumer       升1.9%。儘管各國的經濟表現在數據上錄得
    goods; and in the Eurozone, gross domestic product (“GDP”)           升幅，然而受不利因素，包括經濟活動尤其
    grew by 0.7% year-on-year, retail sales increased by 1.9% year-      是製造業勢頭疲弱，以及整體消費者信心低
    on-year in December. Despite these improvements in economic          迷影響，市場環境仍然充滿不確定性。
    performance across various countries, the market environment
    remained fraught with uncertainty due to unfavourable factors
    such as weak economic activity, particularly in the manufacturing
    industry, and overall low consumer confidence.

    In this complex market environment, the Group completed              在這個複雜的巿場環境下，本集團已完成全
    its global expansion initiatives. The Group’s sales offices and      球佈局部署，旗下各國銷售辦事處及生產基
    production bases in various countries established a strong           地已站穩陣腳，不僅為本集團在各主要巿場
    foothold, providing the Group with a competitive edge in             創造了競爭優勢，更建立了遍佈全球的業
    key markets and creating a worldwide business network                務網絡，有助把握巿場回暖的機遇。本集團
    conducive to seizing opportunities arising from the rebound          位於中國內地、越南及孟加拉等主要成衣出
    of the apparel market. The Group’s production bases in major         口國的生產基地，訂單數量穩步增加。與此
    garment exporting countries such as Mainland China, Vietnam          同時，在中美洲及南歐等地區的新廠房，營
    and Bangladesh have seen a steady increase in order volumes.         運已漸上軌道，並成功開拓了新客戶並獲
    Meanwhile, operations at new factories in Central America and        取 新 訂 單 。 因 此 ， 本 集 團 延 續 了 2024 年 上
    Southern Europe progressed smoothly, securing new customers          半的增長勢頭，全年收益較去年同期上升
    and orders. As a result, the Group maintained its growth             48.0% 至 約 551,049,000 港 元（ 2023 年 ： 約

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                                        風險管理及內部監控

     The Company places great importance on internal control and                                 本公司非常重視內部監控及風險管理，以保
     risk management to safeguard the assets of the Company                                      障本公司資產及股東利益。董事會全面負責
     and the interests of Shareholders. The Board has overall                                    內部監控制度，並檢討其成效。審核委員會
     responsibility for the system of internal control and for reviewing                         協助董事會履行其維持有效內部監控制度之
     its effectiveness. The Audit Committee assists the Board in                                 責任。審核委員會檢討本集團評估其監控環
     meeting its responsibilities for maintaining an effective system                            境之程序及風險評估程序，以及透過本公司
     of internal control. The Audit Committee reviews the process                                內部監控顧問之協助管理業務及監控風險之
     by which the Group evaluates its control environment and risk                               方法。
     assessment process, and the way in which business and control
     risks are managed through the assistance of the Company’s
     Internal Control Consultant.

     The Internal Control Consultant assesses risk exposure,                                     內部監控顧問評估風險，制訂審核計劃及確
     formulates audit plan and ensures that the audit programs cover                             保審核程序以輪替方式涵蓋營運附屬公司之
     key internal control areas of operating subsidiaries on a rotational                        關鍵內部監控範圍，以供審核委員會審閱。
     basis for the review by the Audit Committee.

     The Internal Control Consultant reports their findings and                                  內部監控顧問向本公司管理層及審核委員會
     recommendations to management of the Company and the                                        匯報彼發現事項及建議。內部監控顧問亦監
     Audit C o m m i t t e e . T h e I n t e r n a l C o n t r o l C o n s u l t a n t a l s o   察因應彼建議而協定之跟進行動，並向審核
     monitors the follow-up actions agreed upon in response to                                   委員會報告該等建議之實施進度。
     her recommendations and reports to the Audit Committee the
     progress of implementation of those recommendations.

     With the assistance of the Audit Committee and the Company’s                                在審核委員會及本公司內部監控顧問協助
     Internal Control Consultant, the Board is satisfied that the                                下，董事會信納本集團的整體財務、營運及
     overall financial, operational and compliance controls, and                                 合規監控以及風險管理仍然有效充足。
     risk management of the Group continues to be effective and

–   An EGM shall be convened on the requisition of one or            － 股東特別大會須應一名或多名股東的請求
    more Shareholders holding, at the date of deposit of the           召開，該等股東於提出請求當日須持有本
    requisition, not less than one tenth of the paid up capital of     公司繳足股本不少於十分之一且擁有在股
    the Company (the “Eligible Shareholder(s)”) having the             東大會上投票的權利（「合資格股東」）。
    right of voting at general meetings. Such requisition shall be     有關請求須以書面形式向董事會或公司秘
    made in writing to the Board or the Company Secretary for          書提出，藉以要求董事會就處理有關請求
    the purpose of requiring an EGM to be called by the Board for      所指明之任何事務而召開股東特別大會，
    the transaction of any business specified in such requisition      而有關大會須在送交該請求後兩個月內舉
    and such meeting shall be held within two months after the         行；
    deposit of such requisition;

–   The requisition must state clearly the names of the Eligible     － 請求必須載明相關合資格股東的姓名、召
    Shareholders concerned, specify the objects of the meeting,        開大會的事由，並由相關合資格股東簽
    and be signed by the Eligible Shareholders concerned. The          署。合資格股東必須證明彼等於本公司的
    Eligible Shareholders must prove their shareholdings in the        持股量足以令本公司信納；
    Company to the satisfaction of the Company;

–   The requisition will be verified with Hong Kong branch share     － 請求將由本公司香港股份過戶登記分處核
    registrar and transfer office of the Company and upon their        實，在確定請求為適當及符合程序後，董
    confirmation that the requisition is proper and in order, the      事會將根據組織章程細則的規定向所有登
    Board will convene an EGM by serving sufficient notice             記股東發出充分通知，藉以召開股東特別
    in accordance with the requirements under the Articles             大會。反之，若請求經核實為不符合程序
    of Association to all the registered Shareholders. On the          或有關股東未能繳存足夠款項撥付本公司
    contrary, if the requisition has been verified as not in order     上述用途的開支，有關合資格股東將獲通
    or the Shareholders concerned have failed to deposit               知該結果，因此，董事會將不會要求召開
    sufficient money to meet the Company’s expenses for the            股東特別大會；及
    said purposes, the Eligible Shareholder(s) concerned will be
    advised of this outcome and accordingly, the Board will not
    call for an EGM; and

–   If within 21 days of such deposit, the Board fails to proceed    － 如董事會在有關送交日期起計21日內未有
    to convene such meeting, the requisitionist(s) himself             進行安排召開有關會議，則請求人（或多
    (themselves) may do so in the same manner, and all                 名請求人）可用相同方式自行召開會議，
    reasonable expenses incurred by the requisitionist(s) as a         且請求人因董事會未有妥為召開會議而產
    result of the failure of the Board shall be reimbursed to the      生的所有合理費用須由本公司償付。
    requisitionist(s) by the Company.

    detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is           述或凌駕於內部控制之上，故未能發現因
    higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve      欺詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險較因錯
    collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations,      誤所導致者為高。
    or the override of internal control.

•   Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the       • 瞭解與審核有關的內部控制，以設計在有
    audit in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate      關情況下屬恰當的審計程序，但並非旨在
    in the circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing         對 貴集團內部控制的有效性發表意見。
    an opinion on the effectiveness of the Group’s internal
    control.

•   Evaluate the appropriateness of accounting policies used          • 評估所用會計政策是否恰當，以及董事所
    and the reasonableness of accounting estimates and related          作出的會計估計及相關披露是否合理。
    disclosures made by the directors.

•   Conclude on the appropriateness of the directors’ use of the      • 就董事採用持續經營會計基礎是否恰當作
    going concern basis of accounting and, based on the audit           出總結，並根據已獲取的審核憑證，確定
    evidence obtained, whether a material uncertainty exists            是否有對 貴集團持續經營的能力構成重
    related to events or conditions that may cast significant           大疑問的事件或情況等重大不確定因素。
    doubt on the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern.        倘吾等總結認為存在重大不確定因素，則
    If we conclude that a material uncertainty exists, we are           吾等須於核數師報告中提請注意綜合財務
    required to draw attention in our auditor’s report to the           報表內的相關披露，或倘相關披露不足，
    related disclosures in the consolidated financial statements        則吾等須發表非無保留意見。吾等的結論
    or, if such disclosures are inadequate, to modify our opinion.      為基於直至核數師報告日期所獲取的審
    Our conclusions are based on the audit evidence obtained up         核憑證。然而，未來事件或情況或會導
    to the date of our auditor’s report. However, future events         致 貴集團不再具有持續經營的能力。
    or conditions may cause the Group to cease to continue as a
    going concern.

•   Evaluate the overall presentation, structure and content          • 評估綜合財務報表的整體呈列、架構及內
    of the consolidated financial statements, including the             容（包括披露資料）以及綜合財務報表是
    disclosures, and whether the consolidated financial                 否公平反映相關交易及事項。
    statements represent the underlying transactions and events
    in a manner that achieves fair presentation.

     we have complied with relevant ethical requirements regarding           遵守有關獨立性的相關道德要求，並就或會

     current period and are therefore the key audit matters. We              報告中描述此等事項，除非法律或規例不允
     describe these matters in our auditor’s report unless law or            許公開披露此等事項，或在極端罕見的情況

    2.1 Basis of preparation                                                2.1 編製基準

         These financial statements have been prepared in                     該等財務報表乃按照香港會計師公會
         accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards             （「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港財務
         (“HKFRSs”) (which include all HKFRSs, Hong Kong                      報告準則（「香港財務報告準則」）（包
         Accounting Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations)                  括所有香港財務報告準則、香港會計
         issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public                準則（「香港會計準則」）及詮釋）及香
         Accountants (“HKICPA”) and the disclosure requirements               港公司條例的披露規定編製。
         of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

         These financial statements have been prepared under the              該等財務報表乃按歷史成本法編製，
         historical cost convention, except for a financial asset at          惟按公平值計入損益的金融資產則按
         fair value through profit or loss which has been measured            公平值計量。該等財務報表以港元列
         at fair value. These financial statements are presented              值，除另有指明外，所有數值已約整
         in Hong Kong dollars and all values are rounded to the               至最接近的千位（千港元）。
         nearest thousand (HK$’000) except when otherwise
         indicated.

         Basis of consolidation                                               綜合入賬基準

         The consolidated financial statements include the                   綜合財務報表包括本集團截至2024年
         financial statements of the Group for the year ended                12月31日止年度的財務報表。附屬公
         31 December 2024. A subsidiary is an entity (including              司指由本公司直接或間接控制的實體
         a structured entity), directly or indirectly, controlled            （包括結構性實體）。當本集團通過參
         by the Company. Control is achieved when the Group                  與投資對象的相關活動而承擔可變回
         is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its             報的風險或有權享有可變回報，並且
         involvement with the investee and has the ability to                有能力透過對投資對象的權力（即賦
         affect those returns through its power over the investee            予本集團現有能力以主導投資對象的
         (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current ability      相關活動的現有權利）影響該等回報
         to direct the relevant activities of the investee).                 時，即表示擁有控制權。

         Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of                 一般而言，推定持有大部分投票權者
         voting rights results in control. When the Company has               擁有控制權。當本公司擁有少於投資
         less than a majority of the voting or similar rights of              對象大多數投票權或類似權利，則本
         an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts and              集團於評估其對投資對象是否擁有權
         circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an              力時考慮一切相關事實及情況，包
         investee, including:                                                 括：

         (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote                  (a) 與投資對象其他投票權持有人的
             holders of the investee;                                             合約安排；

         (b) rights arising from other contractual arrangements;              (b) 來自其他合約安排的權利；及
             and

         (c) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.           (c) 本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

     3. MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                         3. 重要會計政策

         Investment in an associate                                           於一間聯營公司的投資

         An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term         聯營公司為本集團於其持有一般附帶不少
         interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting         於20%表決權的長期股本權益，並具有
         rights and over which it has significant influence. Significant      重大影響力的實體。重大影響力指有權參
         influence is the power to participate in the financial and           與投資對象的財務及經營政策決定，惟並
         operating policy decisions of the investee, but is not control       非控制或共同控制該等政策。
         or joint control over those policies.

         The Group’s investment in an associate is stated in the              本集團於一間聯營公司的投資乃按本集
         consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s          團根據權益會計法應佔資產淨值減任何減
         share of net assets under the equity method of accounting,           值虧損於綜合財務狀況表內列賬。本集團
         less any impairment losses. The Group’s share of the post-           應佔聯營公司收購後業績及其他全面收益
         acquisition results and other comprehensive income of                分別計入綜合損益表及綜合其他全面收益
         associates is included in the consolidated statement of              表。此外，倘於聯營公司直接確認的權益
         profit or loss and consolidated other comprehensive income,          出現變動，則本集團將於綜合權益變動表
         respectively. In addition, when there has been a change              內確認其應佔任何變動（如適用）。本集
         recognised directly in the equity of the associate, the Group        團與其聯營公司之間交易的未變現收益及
         recognises its share of any changes, when applicable, in             虧損將以本集團於聯營公司的投資為限對
         the consolidated statement of changes in equity. Unrealised          銷，惟未變現虧損提供所轉讓資產減值的
         gains and losses resulting from transactions between the             憑證除外。收購該聯營公司所產生的商譽
         Group and its associate are eliminated to the extent of              計入本集團於聯營公司的投資一部分。
         the Group’s investment in the associate, except where
         unrealised losses provide evidence of an impairment of the
         assets transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition of
         the associate is included as part of the Group’s investment in
         an associate.

         If an investment in an associate becomes an investment               倘於聯營公司的投資成為於合營企業的
         in a joint venture or vice versa, the retained interest is           投資或出現相反情況，保留權益將不予重
         not remeasured. Instead, the investment continues to be              新計量。反之，該投資繼續根據權益法入
         accounted for under the equity method. In all other case,            賬。在所有其他情況下，於失去對聯營公
         upon loss of significant influence over the associate or joint       司的重大影響力或失去對合營企業的共同
         control over the joint venture, the Group measures and               控制權時，本集團按其公平值計量及確認
         recognises any retained investment at its fair value. Any            任何保留投資。於失去重大影響力或共同
         difference between the carrying amount of the associate or           控制權時，聯營公司或合營企業的賬面值
         joint venture upon loss of significant influence or joint control    與保留投資及出售所得款項的公平值之間
         and the fair value of the retained investment and proceeds           的任何差額於損益中確認。
         from disposal is recognised in profit or loss.

    Business combinations and goodwill                                 業務合併及商譽

    Business combinations are accounted for using the                  業務合併使用收購法入賬。轉讓代價按
    acquisition method. The consideration transferred is               收購日期公平值計量，即本集團所轉讓資
    measured at the acquisition date fair value which is the sum       產、本集團承擔的被收購方前擁有人負債
    of the acquisition date fair values of assets transferred by       與本集團為換取被收購方控股權而發行的
    the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group to the former          股本權益於收購日期公平值的總和。就各
    owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued by          項業務合併而言，本集團選擇是否按公平
    the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree. For each        值或佔被收購方的可識別淨資產的比例計
    business combination, the Group elects whether to measure          量於被收購方的非控股權益。非控股權益
    the non-controlling interests in the acquiree at fair value or     的所有其他部分按公平值計量。收購相關
    at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable net      成本於產生時支銷。
    assets. All other components of non-controlling interests
    are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are
    expensed as incurred.

    The Group determines that it has acquired a business when          當本集團收購一組業務及資產，而當中包
    the acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and    括共同對創造產出的能力具有重大貢獻的
    a substantive process that together significantly contribute       一項投入和實質性過程時，則確定其已收
    to the ability to create outputs.                                  購一項業務。

    When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合約條
    financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate           款、於收購日期的經濟狀況及相關條件評
    classification and designation in accordance with the              估所承擔的金融資產及負債，以作出適當
    contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent            分類及指定，包括將被收購方所訂立的主
    conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the           合約中的嵌入式衍生工具拆分。
    separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the
    acquiree.

    If the business combination is achieved in stages, the             倘業務合併分階段進行，則先前持有的股
    previously held equity interest is remeasured at its               權按其於收購日期的公平值重新計量，而
    acquisition date fair value and any resulting gain or loss is      任何所導致的收益或虧損乃於損益確認。
    recognised in profit or loss.

    Fair value measurement                                               公平值計量

    The Group measures its financial asset at fair value through         本集團於各報告期末按公平值計量其按公
    profit or loss at fair value at the end of each reporting period.    平值計入損益的金融資產。公平值指於計
    Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an            量日期市場參與者之間的有序交易中為出
    asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction      售資產而收取或轉讓負債而支付的價格。
    between market participants at the measurement date.                 公平值計量乃基於出售資產或轉讓負債之
    The fair value measurement is based on the presumption               交易於該資產或負債之主要市場或（倘並
    that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability     無主要市場）就該資產或負債而言最具優
    takes place either in the principal market for the asset or          勢的市場進行之假設。主要或最具優勢市
    liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most      場須為本集團可進入的市場。資產或負債
    advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal        的公平值乃使用市場參與者將資產或負債
    or the most advantageous market must be accessible by the            定價時使用的假設計量（假設市場參與者
    Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured         依照彼等的最佳經濟利益行事）。
    using the assumptions that market participants would use
    when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market
    participants act in their economic best interest.

    A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into         非金融資產的公平值計量考慮市場參與者
    account a market participant’s ability to generate economic          於資產最高及最佳使用狀態時使用或將其
    benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by        出售予另一個市場參與者所產生經濟利益
    selling it to another market participant that would use the          能力。
    asset in its highest and best use.

                                                                                  （續）

         The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in          本集團使用適用於有關情況且具備充足可
         the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available        供使用數據以計量公平值的估值技術，以
         to measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant                盡可能使用相關可觀察輸入值及盡可能減
         observable inputs and minimising the use of unobservable             少使用不可觀察輸入值。
         inputs.

         All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured          於財務報表計量或披露公平值的所有資產
         or disclosed in the financial statements are categorised             及負債均根據對公平值計量整體而言屬重
         within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows, based         大的最低層輸入值按下述公平值層級進行
         on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value      分類：
         measurement as a whole:

          Level 1 –     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active          第一級 － 基 於 相 同 資 產 或 負 債 於 活 躍
                        markets for identical assets or liabilities                  市場的報價（未經調整）

          Level 2 –     based on valuation techniques for which the            第二級 － 基 於 可 直 接 或 間 接 觀 察 就 公
                        lowest level input that is significant to the fair           平值計量而言屬重大的最低
                        value measurement is observable, either directly             層輸入值的估值技術
                        or indirectly

          Level 3 –     based on valuation techniques for which the            第三級 － 基 於 無 法 觀 察 就 公 平 值 計 量
                        lowest level input that is significant to the fair           而言屬重大的最低層輸入值
                        value measurement is unobservable                            的估值技術

         For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial      就經常於財務報表確認的資產及負債而
         statements on a recurring basis, the Group determines                言，於各報告期末，本集團通過重新評估
         whether transfers have occurred between levels in the                分類釐定各層級之間是否發生轉移（基於
         hierarchy by reassessing categorisation (based on the lowest         對公平值計量整體而言屬重大的最低層級
         level input that is significant to the fair value measurement as     的輸入）。
         a whole) at the end of each reporting period.

3. MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                              3. 重要會計政策
                                                                                 （續）

    Impairment of non-financial assets                                    非金融資產減值

    Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual              倘有跡象顯示存在減值，或當需要對一項
    impairment testing for an asset is required (other than               資產（存貨、遞延稅項資產及金融資產除
    inventories, deferred tax assets and financial assets),               外）作年度減值測試，則估計資產之可收
    the asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s               回金額。資產之可收回金額為資產或現金
    recoverable amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-              產生單位之使用價值以及其公平值減出售
    generating unit’s value in use and its fair value less costs of       成本之較高者計算，並就個別資產而釐
    disposal, and is determined for an individual asset, unless           定，惟倘資產並未能產生大致獨立於其他
    the asset does not generate cash inflows that are largely             資產或資產組別的現金流入，則就資產所
    independent of those from other assets or groups of assets,           屬之現金產生單位釐定可收回金額。
    in which case the recoverable amount is determined for the
    cash-generating unit to which the asset belongs.

    An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount          減值虧損僅於資產之賬面值超過其可收回
    of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing              金額時確認。於評估使用價值時，估計未
    value in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted          來現金流量乃採用反映當時市場對貨幣時
    to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that             間價值以及資產特定風險的除稅前貼現率
    reflects current market assessments of the time value of              貼現至其現值。減值虧損按與該減值資產
    money and the risks specific to the asset. An impairment              之功能相符的開支類別於產生期間自損益
    loss is charged to profit or loss in the period in which it arises    扣除。

    the impaired asset.

    have decreased. If such an indication exists, the recoverable         出估計。先前確認的資產（商譽除外）減
    amount is estimated. A previously recognised impairment               值虧損僅於用以釐定資產之可收回金額之

    A reversal of such an impairment loss is credited to profit or

    Initial recognition and measurement                                  初步確認及計量

    loss.

    The classification of financial assets at initial recognition        金融資產於初步確認時的分類視乎金融資
    depends on the financial asset’s contractual cash flow               產合約現金流量的特徵及本集團管理該等
    characteristics and the Group’s business model for managing          資產的業務模式而定。除不包含重大融資
    them. With the exception of trade receivables that do not            部分或本集團已應用可行權宜方法不就重
    contain a significant financing component or for which the           大融資部分的影響作出調整的貿易應收款
    Group has applied the practical expedient of not adjusting           項外，本集團初步按其公平值加（倘並非
    the effect of a significant financing component, the Group           按公平值計入損益的金融資產）交易成本
    initially measures a financial asset at its fair value, plus in      計量金融資產。依據下文「收益確認」所
    the case of a financial asset not at fair value through profit or    載政策，並無重大融資部分或本集團並未
    loss, transaction costs. Trade receivables that do not contain       就此應用可行權宜方法的貿易應收款項按
    a significant financing component or for which the Group             照香港財務報告準則第15號釐定的交易
    has applied the practical expedient are measured at the              價格計量。
    transaction price determined under HKFRS 15 in accordance
    with the policies set out for “Revenue recognition” below.

    In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured         為使金融資產按攤銷成本或按公平值計入
    at amortised cost or fair value through other comprehensive          其他全面收益分類及計量，需產生僅為支
    income, it needs to give rise to cash flows that are solely          付本金及未償還本金利息（「僅為支付本

    amount outstanding. Financial assets with cash flows that            為何，現金流量並非僅為支付本金及利息
    are not SPPI are classified and measured at fair value through       的金融資產乃按公平值計入損益分類及計
    profit or loss, irrespective of the business model.                  量。

         The Group’s business model for managing financial assets           本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指為產
         refers to how it manages its financial assets in order to          生現金流量管理金融資產的方式。業務
         generate cash flows. The business model determines                 模式釐定現金流量會否因收取合約現金
         whether cash flows will result from collecting contractual         流 量 、 出 售 金 融 資 產（ 或 以 上 兩 者 ）而
         cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both. Financial       產生。持有按攤銷成本分類及計量的金
         assets classified and measured at amortised cost are               融資產的業務模式旨在收取合約現金流
         held within a business model with the objective to hold            量，而持有按公平值計入其他全面收益
         financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows,       分類及計量的金融資產的業務模式旨在
         while financial assets classified and measured at fair value       收取合約現金流量及出售金融資產。並
         through other comprehensive income are held within a               非按上述業務模式持有的金融資產按公
         business model with the objective of both holding to collect       平值計量計入損益入賬分類及計量。
         contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which
         are not held within the aforementioned business models are
         classified and measured at fair value through profit or loss.

         Purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery of    購買或出售金融資產須於市場規例或慣
         assets within the period generally established by regulation       例規定的期間內交付資產，乃於交易日
         or convention in the marketplace are recognised on the trade       （即本集團承諾購買或出售資產的日期）
         date, that is, the date that the Group commits to purchase or      確認。
         sell the asset.

         on their classification as follows:                                而定：

         Financial assets at amortised cost (debt instruments)              按攤銷成本計量的金融資產（債務工具）

         Financial assets at amortised cost are subsequently                按攤銷成本計量的金融資產其後使用實
         measured using the effective interest method and are               際利率法計量，並可予減值。收益及虧
         subject to impairment. Gains and losses are recognised             損於資產終止確認、修改或減值時於損
         in the statement of profit or loss when the asset is               益表中確認。
         derecognised, modified or impaired.

    General approach                                                    一般方式

    ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures             預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自首次
    for which there has not been a significant increase in credit       確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險而言，預
    risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit        期信貸虧損乃就於未來12個月內可能發
    losses that result from default events that are possible            生違約事件而導致的信貸虧損計提撥備
    within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit        （12個月預期信貸虧損）。就自初步確認
    exposures for which there has been a significant increase           起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而言，不論何
    in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss allowance is       時發生違約，於餘下風險年期內的預期信
    required for credit losses expected over the remaining life         貸虧損均須計提虧損撥備（全期預期信貸
    of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a       虧損）。
    lifetime ECL).

    At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                  於各報告日期，本集團於評估自初步確認
    the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased             後金融工具的信貸風險是否顯著增加時，
    significantly since initial recognition. When making the            本集團將於報告日期金融工具發生違約的
    assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default                風險與於初步確認日期金融工具發生違約
    occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting date      的風險進行比較，並考慮合理且具有理據
    with the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument    支持的資料，包括無需付出不必要的成本
    as at the date of initial recognition and considers reasonable      或努力而可得的過往及前瞻性資料。本集
    and supportable information that is available without undue         團認為當合約付款逾期90天以上，信貸
    cost or effort, including historical and forward-looking            風險會大幅增加。
    information. The Group considers that there has been a
    significant increase in credit risk when contractual payments
    are more than 90 days past due.

    The Group considers a financial asset in default when               本集團將合約付款逾期90天的金融資產
    contractual payments are 90 days past due. However,                 視作違約。然而，於若干情況下，當內部
    in certain cases, the Group may also consider a financial           或外部資料顯示本集團不可能在本集團採
    asset to be in default when internal or external information        取任何信貸增強措施前悉數收回未償還合
    indicates that the Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding     約金額時，本集團亦可能認為該金融資產
    contractual amounts in full before taking into account any          違約。當並無合理預期可收回合約現金流
    credit enhancements held by the Group. A financial asset            量時，金融資產將予以撇銷。
    is written off when there is no reasonable expectation of
    recovering the contractual cash flows.

                   that are not credit-impaired financial assets and               減值金融資產且其虧損撥備
                   for which the loss allowance is measured at an                  乃按相當於全期預期信貸虧
                   amount equal to lifetime ECLs                                   損的金額計量

                   lifetime ECLs                                                   金額計量

         Simplified approach                                                 簡化方式

         For trade receivables that do not contain a significant             就不包括重大融資部分的貿易應收款項而
         financing component or when the Group applies the practical         言，或當本集團應用可行權宜方法不就重

         calculating ECLs. Under the simplified approach, the Group          方式，本集團不會追蹤信貸風險變動，惟

         date. The Group has established a provision matrix that             損經驗訂立撥備矩陣，並按債務人及經濟
         is based on its historical credit loss experience, adjusted         環境具體的前瞻性因素作出調整。
         for forward-looking factors specific to the debtors and the

         component and lease receivables, the Group chooses as               及租賃應收款項而言，本集團採納簡化方

         Derecognition of financial liabilities                              終止確認金融負債

         A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under     當金融負債項下的責任被解除或取消或到
         the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.               期，則終止確認金融負債。

         When an existing financial liability is replaced by another         倘現有金融負債由同一貸款人授予條款顯
         from the same lender on substantially different terms, or the       著不同的另一項負債取代，或現有負債的
         terms of an existing liability are substantially modified, such     條款經重大修訂，則有關交換或修訂視作
         an exchange or modification is treated as a derecognition of        終止確認原有負債及確認新負債，其賬面
         the original liability and a recognition of a new liability, and    值的差額於損益表確認。
         the difference between the respective carrying amounts is
         recognised in the statement of profit or loss.

         Offsetting of financial instruments                                 抵銷金融工具

         Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the       倘現時存在一項可合法強制執行權利可抵
         net amount is reported in the statement of financial position       銷已確認金額，且有意以淨額基準結算或
         if there is a currently enforceable legal right to offset the       同時變現資產及償付債務，則金融資產及
         recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle on           金融負債可予抵銷，而淨額則於財務狀況
         a net basis, or to realise the assets and settle the liabilities    表中列報。
         simultaneously.

         Inventories                                                         存貨

         Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable      存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者的較低者入
         value. Cost is determined on the weighted average basis             賬。成本以加權平均基準計算，如屬在製
         and, in the case of work in progress and finished goods,            品及製成品，則包括直接物料成本、直接
         comprises direct materials, direct labour and an appropriate        勞工成本及適當比例的經常性開支。可變
         proportion of overheads. Net realisable value is based on           現淨值按估計售價減完成及出售所產生的
         estimated selling prices less any estimated costs to be             任何估計成本釐定。
         incurred to completion and disposal.

         Cash and cash equivalents                                           現金及現金等價物

         Cash and cash equivalents in the statement of financial             財務狀況表中的現金及現金等價物包括手
         position comprise cash on hand and at banks, and short-             頭現金及銀行存款以及可隨時轉換為已知
         term highly liquid deposits with a maturity of generally            數額現金、價值變動風險極微且持作滿足
         within three months that are readily convertible into known         短期現金承擔的一般於三個月內到期的短
         amounts of cash, subject to an insignificant risk of changes        期高流動性存款。
         in value and held for the purpose of meeting short-term cash
         commitments.

         For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash flows,        就綜合現金流量表而言，現金及現金等價
         cash and cash equivalents comprise cash on hand and at              物包括手頭現金及銀行存款以及上文界定
         banks, and short-term deposits as defined above, less bank          的短期存款，減去須於要求時償還的銀行
         overdrafts which are repayable on demand and form an                透支，為本集團現金管理不可分割的一部
         integral part of the Group’s cash management.                       分。

    Provisions                                                         撥備

    A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or      當因過往事件導致現有法律或推定責任，
    constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is     而日後可能須動用資源履行有關責任，且
    probable that a future outflow of resources will be required       有關責任所涉數額能可靠估計，則須確認
    to settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can    撥備。
    be made of the amount of the obligation.

    When the effect of discounting is material, the amount             當貼現影響重大時，已確認的撥備金額為
    recognised for a provision is the present value at the end         預期解除責任所需的未來支出於報告期末
    of the reporting period of the future expenditures expected        的現值。隨時間流逝而產生的貼現現值增
    to be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the       加於損益入賬列作融資成本。
    discounted present value amount arising from the passage of
    time is included in finance costs in profit or loss.

    Income tax                                                         所得稅

    Income tax comprises current and deferred tax. Income              所得稅包括即期及遞延稅項。有關損益以
    tax relating to items recognised outside profit or loss            外確認項目的所得稅在損益外於其他全面
    is recognised outside profit or loss, either in other              收益或直接於股本確認。
    comprehensive income or directly in equity.

    Current tax assets and liabilities are measured at the amount      即期稅項資產及負債按預期可自稅務機構
    expected to be recovered from or paid to the taxation              收回或支付予稅務機構的金額計量，稅率
    authorities, based on tax rates (and tax laws) that have been      乃按於報告期末已實施或實際上已實施的
    enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting       稅率（及稅法）計算，並會考慮本集團經
    period, taking into consideration interpretations and practices    營所在國家的現行詮釋及慣例。
    prevailing in the countries in which the Group operates.

    Deferred tax is provided, using the liability method, on all       遞延稅項乃就資產及負債的稅基與其用作
    temporary differences at the end of the reporting period           財務報告用途的賬面值之間於報告期末的
    between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their          所有暫時差額採用負債法作出撥備。
    carrying amounts for financial reporting purposes.

          Share-based payments                                               以股份為基礎付款

          The Company operates a share option scheme. Employees               本公司設立購股權計劃。本集團僱員（包
          (including directors) of the Group receive remuneration in the      括董事）會按以股份為基礎付款方式收取
          form of share-based payments, whereby employees render              酬金，而僱員會提供服務以換取股本工具

          measured by reference to the fair value at the date at which       授出當日的公平值計算。公平值乃由外部
          they are granted. The fair value is determined by an external      估值師使用二項模型釐定，進一步詳情載
          valuer using a binomial model, further details of which are        於財務報表附註29。
          given in note 29 to the financial statements.

          The cost of Equity-Settled Transactions is recognised in           股本結算交易的成本連同權益的相應升幅
          employee benefit expense, together with a corresponding            會於達到表現及╱或服務條件的期間於僱
          increase in equity, over the period in which the performance       員福利開支確認。於歸屬日前各報告期末
          and/or service conditions are fulfilled. The cumulative            就股本結算交易確認的累計開支反映歸屬
          expense recognised for Equity-Settled Transactions at              期已屆滿部份及本集團對最終將歸屬的股
          the end of each reporting period until the vesting date            本工具數目所作的最佳估計。損益表在某
          reflects the extent to which the vesting period has expired        一期間內所錄得扣減或進賬乃反映於期初

          instruments that will ultimately vest. The charge or credit to
          the statement of profit or loss for a period represents the

          beginning and end of that period.

          Service and non-market performance conditions are not              釐定獎勵的授出日期公平值時不會計及服
          taken into account when determining the grant date fair            務及非市場表現條件，惟會評估達成該等

          met is assessed as part of the Group’s best estimate of            的股本工具數量的最佳估計。市場表現條
          the number of equity instruments that will ultimately vest.        件於授出日期公平值內反映。獎勵所附帶

    disclosures, and the disclosure of contingent liabilities.            露。有關此等假設及估計的不確定因素可
    Uncertainty about these assumptions and estimates could               能產生的結果或會導致未來受影響的資產
    result in outcomes that could require a material adjustment           或負債賬面金額須作出重大調整。
    to the carrying amounts of the assets or liabilities affected in
    the future.

    significant effect on the amounts recognised in the financial
    statements:

    Determining the method to estimate variable consideration             釐定估計可變代價的方法及評估出售印刷
    and assessing the constraint for the sale of printing products        產品的限制

    Certain contracts for the sale of printing products include           若干出售印刷產品的合約包括產生可變代
    a right of return that give rises to variable consideration. In       價的退貨權利。於估計可變代價時，本集
    estimating the variable consideration, the Group is required          團須視乎何種方法可更能預測其有權收取
    to use either the expected value method or the most likely            的代價金額，以決定使用預期價值法或最
    amount method based on which method better predicts the               可能金額法。
    amount of consideration to which it will be entitled.

    The Group determined that the expected value method is                鑒於大量客戶合約之特性相似，本集團確
    the appropriate method to use in estimating the variable              定預期價值法對用於估計附帶退貨權之工

    In estimating the variable consideration for the sale                 於估計銷售工業產品（隨帶批量回扣）的
    of industrial products with volume rebates, the Group                 可變代價時，本集團認定採用最可能的金

    appropriate. The selected method that better predicts the             相關可變代價金額的方法主要受合約設定

                                                                                 （續）

    Provision for expected credit losses on trade receivables             貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損撥備（續）
    (Continued)

    The assessment of the correlation among historical observed           對歷史觀察違約率、預測經濟狀況及預期

    Company by resolution in general meeting, the Scheme will           式終止，該計劃將自該日期起計10年內
    remain in force for 10 years from that date.                        維持有效。

      30. RESERVES                                                           30. 儲備

          The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements                本集團於本年度及過往年度的儲備及其變
          therein for current and prior years are presented in the             動金額於財務報表第62頁的綜合權益變
          consolidated statement of changes in equity on page 62 of            動表呈列。
          the financial statements.

          (a) Merger reserve                                                   (a) 合併儲備

               The merger reserve represents the aggregate of the paid-           合併儲備指於重組前控股股東應佔現
               up share capital of the subsidiaries now comprising the            時組成本集團的附屬公司之繳足股本
               Group attributable to the Controlling Shareholders prior to        總額。
               the Reorganisation.

               The balance of the merger reserve at 31 December 2024              於2024年及2023年12月31日的合併
               and 2023 mainly represented the deemed distribution                儲備結餘主要指根據重組透過發行本
               to the Controlling Shareholders by issuance of the                 公司股份視作向控股股東的分派。
               Company’s shares pursuant to the Reorganisation.

          (b) Exchange fluctuation reserve                                     (b) 外匯波動儲備

               The exchange fluctuation reserve comprises all relevant            外匯波動儲備包括換算海外業務財務
               exchange differences arising from the translation of the           報表而產生的所有相關匯兌差額。
               financial statements of foreign operations.

          (c) Share premium                                                    (c) 股份溢價

               The share premium represents the difference between                股份溢價指就重組而言已發行股份的
               the par value of the shares issued and the consideration           面值與已付代價之間的差額。
               paid for the Reorganisation.
